# Treatment Market Prestige V3 — Round 5 Results

1. **Round 5 study ID:** `"treatment-market-prestige-v3-r5-63a7fdee93518f74"`

2. **Hierarchical methodology:** `"Transparent empirical-Bayes random-effects meta-regression on controlled set-treatment pseudo-effects; DerSimonian-Laird tau\u00b2 and precision-weighted partial pooling."`

3. **Shrinkage/partial-pooling method:** `"lambda=tau\u00b2/(tau\u00b2+sampling variance); small/noisy cells shrink more strongly toward the universe population effect."`
